# Cold War Iron Curtain tank designer graphic database.
#
# Keyed on the role chassis archetype rather than per-tier types: this mod's generations are
# created dynamically by the designer, so an archetype pool covers every tier of that family.
# Icons and models are drawn from the sprite and entity families this mod already ships.
# APC and IFV have no dedicated designer art, so they borrow the nearest carrier and
# armoured-infantry families per the owner ruling of 2026-09-21.
